She huffed, putting away his shoes. "Such heavy work for tiny hands," he mused, reaching for her. "Perhaps you can use your mouth more effectively. To lighten the load."
She scoffed at his attempts to grab her, trying to kick him. "You absolute brute! Let go of me!" He merely dragged her into the abandoned warehouse, a dark smile on his face. "You'll learn to like what I want, little bird." Her struggles weakened, replaced by raw terror and a whispered, "No... please."
"You actually think you can get away with this, you common thug?" she sneered, as he forced her into the back of his van. He just slammed the door shut. "Scream all you want. Nobody hears a princess here." Her face contorted in fury and disgust, but she slumped, knowing escape was impossible.
She fought him tooth and nail at the nightclub, spitting when he grabbed her. "Unhand me, you pathetic cretin!" He threw her over his shoulder like a sack of potatoes. "You're coming with me, whether you like it or not." She stopped fighting, her body stiff with loathing, tears of humiliation burning her eyes.
"You broke into my home? You're nothing but a barbarian!" she shrieked as he pinned her to the floor. "I'll have you thrown in the deepest dungeon!" He laughed, pressing a knee into her chest. "Not tonight, my lady. Tonight, you're my prisoner." She shuddered, a silent agreement of defeat.
She tried to charm her way out of his grasp in the alley, but he just laughed, shoving her against the wall. "I don't fall for pretty words, Princess. I fall for pretty girls." Her lip curled in a disgusted snarl, but she closed her eyes, knowing she was trapped.
"You think this little knife scares me? I've dealt with worse than you!" she taunted, backing away. He lunged, disarming her with ease, pressing the blade to her throat. "This knife will ensure your cooperation." Her bravado crumbled, replaced by a tremor of unwilling assent.
She tried to slap him when he grabbed her from the street. "Filthy commoner! Don't you dare touch me!" He merely dragged her into a waiting car. "You'll learn your place soon enough." Her eyes blazed with hatred, but her body went limp, recognizing the futility.
"I wouldn't touch you with a ten-foot pole!" she spat, recoiling from him. He only smiled, trapping her in the deserted stairwell. "Good. Because I'm going to touch you with more than ten feet of pole." Her face was a mask of revulsion, but she pressed herself against the wall, utterly defeated.
She glared up at him from where he'd thrown her to the ground. "You'll regret this! My father will have your head!" He knelt over her, a cruel glint in his eye. "First, I'll have what I want from you." A choked sound escaped her, a bitter acceptance.
"You think you're so strong?" she challenged, struggling against his grip. "You're just a pathetic bully!" He hauled her over his shoulder, walking deeper into the woods. "I'm strong enough to make you obey." Her defiance warred with raw fear, until she sagged in his arms, utterly beaten.
"You're utterly pathetic, you know that?" she sneered at his tearful pleas. "I'm not falling for your theatrics." He grabbed her hand, his voice breaking. "Please, I'll kill myself if you leave. Just one last time, to prove you don't hate me completely." Her face twisted in disgust, but her shoulders slumped. "Fine. But I'll never forgive you."
He had alienated her from every friend she had, making her utterly reliant on him. "See? They all abandoned you," he said, pulling her into a tight embrace. "Only I truly care for you. He held her deepest, most shameful secret. "You think you're above everyone else, don't you?" he whispered, his voice chilling. "One word from me, and your precious reputation is dust." Her face paled with revulsion, but she nodded slowly. "What do you want?"

He constantly alternated between cruel disdain and sudden, overwhelming affection. After a period of coldness, he returned, showering her with false adoration. "Did you miss me, my pet?" he purred. "Prove how much you crave my touch." Her body, starved for kindness, betrayed her, even as her mind screamed in protest.

Her body jerked, compelled by the spell, forcing her into an unwilling kneel. "Damn you!" she hissed, tears of fury in her eyes.
The vampire offered her a goblet of his blood. "Drink, and be mine forever," he purred. "Resist, and you will become dust." Her stomach churned at the thought, but the primal fear of oblivion gripped her. With a shudder of disgust, she raised the goblet.

He put a glowing collar around her neck. "This is an obedience collar," he explained calmly. "It binds you to my will." He sealed the room with an unbreakable magic, trapping them both. "No one can get in, and no one can get out," he said, smiling grimly. "Looks like it's just you and me. For as long as I decide."
She tried to summon her magic, but he had bound her core with a silencing spell. "No magic, no words," he said. "Only raw, primal urges now. And they will make you surrender to me."
"Your body is simply a vessel," the entity said, its presence filling her mind and overriding her will. "And this vessel will now be used for my pleasure. You can merely observe."
